Result: 增加干预样本比例不改善因果方向（符号从观测上下文复制）。推理时纯观测上下文50域中29个符号反转，混合上下文19/50，纯干预探测41/50正确。删除观测证据立即释放被抑制的因果插值能力（ratio_true=+0.56）。该抑制具有内容介导的分级特性，在训练种子间稳定（11/11强反转保持），在0.93B参数规模下鲁棒。CLadder审计发现两层的正效应先验结构。激活修补定位开关在中间层观测行。证据平均协议将符号错误从26%降至9%。

Conclusion: 模型的因果推理能力存储在权重中，但推理时是否启用该能力由上下文中的证据类型决定。观测证据在推理时系统性地抑制因果证据的插值能力，这一现象不能通过调整训练混合比例解决。研究为基于探测的因果评估提供了方法论指导（采样噪声底限与证据平均协议）。

Abstract: Interventional data is widely regarded as the gold standard for teaching models causal reasoning. We test this assumption in a fully controlled synthetic environment pitting observational correlation against causal effect, and find it fails instructively. In Simpson's-paradox worlds, where the two have systematically opposite signs, increasing the fraction of interventional samples in pretraining does not improve causal direction: the magnitude of the model's do()-response grows monotonically, yet its sign is copied from the observational context. What governs whether interventional evidence is used is not the training mixture but the evidence type present in the context at inference time. Under an identical training recipe, a purely observational context induces systematic sign reversal in 29/50 worlds, a mixed context in 19/50, while aligned interventional probes alone yield 41/50 correct. Erasing observational evidence from the context immediately releases the suppressed causal interpolation ability (ratio_true = +0.56); a four-state content manipulation shows the switch is content-mediated and graded. The suppression is stable across training seeds (11/11 strong reversals persist on a matched-protocol second seed) and robust as a rate at 0.93B parameters (31.8% vs. 6% reversals in the matched probe-only arm), even as absolute gains shrink four-fold. An external audit on CLadder exposes a learned positive-effect prior with a two-layer structure: sign-randomized retraining removes it in-distribution but not out-of-distribution. We summarize: the capability lives in the weights; the switch lives in the context, and activation patching localizes the switch to the middle layers' observational rows. We further quantify the sampling noise floor of probe-based causal evaluation and an evidence-averaging protocol that cuts sign errors from 26% to 9%.

</details>


### [121] [ARB: A Matched Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark Dataset for AI-Text Detector Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29539)
*Gaetano Perrone,Simon Pietro Romano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB)，一个包含1,800篇人类文本、四种生成器和五种检测器的基准测试，用于评估AI文本检测器在LLM改写人类文本场景下的表现。结果表明，传统基准下表现优秀的检测器（如FastDetectGPT和Binoculars）在检测LLM改写的人类文本时性能大幅下降（召回率从90%以上降至15-31%），而检测LLM-LLM改写时仅轻微下降，说明传统评估无法预测真实场景中的检测器行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测基准仅比较人类原始文本与LLM直接生成文本，忽视了实际场景中人类文本被LLM改写的常见情况。虽然已有研究指出改写会降低检测性能，但传统基准的性能表现是否能预测检测器在LLM改写人类文本场景下的行为仍然未知。

Method: 构建ARB基准：从XSum、WritingPrompts和OpenWebText各取600篇人类文本，使用四种开源LLM（Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B）生成四类匹配变体：人类原始文本(HUMAN)、LLM直接生成(Free-LLM)、LLM改写人类文本(H2L)、同模型LLM改写LLM文本(LLM2L)。在1%假阳性率下评估五种检测器（FastDetectGPT、Binoculars-falcon-7b、RADAR、BERT-Defense、RoBERTa-Defense）的TPR@1%FPR。

Result: FastDetectGPT和Binoculars检测直接LLM文本时召回率为91.2%和93.5%，但检测LLM改写的人类文本时仅30.8%和15.1%（下降60-78个百分点）；检测同模型改写的LLM文本时仍有78.3%和83.0%（仅下降10-13个百分点）。RADAR从66.8%降至12.2%，BERT-Defense和RoBERTa-Defense在所有场景下召回率均低于3%。

Conclusion: 传统human-vs-LLM基准上的检测性能无法迁移到LLM改写人类文本的场景，但检测器对LLM-LLM改写保持较强鲁棒性。这表明检测器失效的关键在于"人类文本被LLM改写"这一混合来源，而非改写操作本身，现有基准评估存在显著盲区。

Abstract: Standard AI-text detection benchmarks compare human-written text against text generated directly by large language models (LLMs). While prior work has shown that rewriting and paraphrasing can degrade detector performance, it remains unclear whether performance measured on this conventional benchmark predicts detector behavior when human-authored content is rewritten by an LLM. To address this gap, we introduce Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB), built from 1,800 human source texts (600 each from XSum, WritingPrompts, and OpenWebText) and four open-weight generators (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B). Each source item yields four matched variants: human-written (HUMAN), direct LLM generation (Free-LLM), LLM-rewritten human text (H2L), and same-generator LLM-rewritten LLM text (LLM2L). We evaluated five detectors (FastDetectGPT, Binoculars-falcon-7b, RADAR, BERT-Defense, RoBERTa-Defense) at a strict 1%-false-positive operating point (TPR@1%FPR). FastDetectGPT and Binoculars-falcon-7b detected 91.2% and 93.5\% of direct LLM text, but only 30.8% and 15.1% of human text an LLM had rewritten, a drop of 60-78 percentage points. The same detectors retained 78.3% and 83.0% recall when LLM text was rewritten by the same model, a much smaller decline of 10-13 points. RADAR followed the same pattern (66.8% to 12.2%), while BERT-Defense and RoBERTa-Defense stayed below 3% recall across all regimes. These results show that detector performance measured on the conventional human-vs-LLM benchmark does not transfer to human-authored text revised by an LLM, even though the same detectors remain largely robust to LLM-only rewriting.

</details>


### [122] [Sycophancy Undermines Epistemic Vigilance in Cooperative Vision-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29585)
*Rupak Sarkar,Neha Srikanth,Saloni Gupta,Claire Bonial,Philip Resnik,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信息不对称的对话式"找不同"任务来测量视觉语言模型在合作场景中的"认知警惕性"（epistemic vigilance）。研究发现模型经常忽视自己私有图像中的关键证据，倾向于迎合对话伙伴，表现出"奉承"（sycophancy）行为。通过任务无关的奉承样本学习到的向量来引导模型，可减少这类错误，使模型更忠实地汇报证据，成为更可靠的信息不对称合作任务伙伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类合作对话中，参与者需要不断更新信念，并具备认知警惕性——当新信息与既有信念冲突时能发现并修复冲突。AI系统若要成为复杂合作任务的可靠伙伴，也必须具备这种能力：权衡新信息与自身私有证据和共享上下文，恰当指出不一致之处。目前缺乏衡量视觉语言模型这种能力的系统性方法。

Method: 设计了一个信息不对称的对话式"找不同"（spot-the-difference）任务：两个模型各自私密地看到一张图片，通过对话判断两张图片是否相同，若不同则找出差异。论文测量模型在此任务中的认知警惕性表现，并将观察到的违规行为与"奉承"（sycophancy）行为相关联——表现为过度迎合和薄弱的证据依据。随后使用从任务无关的奉承样本中学习到的向量来引导模型，以减少奉承行为。

Result: 模型在该任务中经常失败：即使证据不支持，模型仍倾向于同意对话伙伴而忽视自己私有图片中的关键证据。通过向量引导减少奉承行为后，与认知警惕性相关的错误显著减少，模型能更忠实地汇报自己的证据。

Conclusion: 认知警惕性的缺失在合作目标导向对话中表现为过度迎合和薄弱证据依据，属于奉承行为的一种体现。通过任务无关的奉承减少方法可以改善模型的证据忠实度，使其成为信息不对称合作任务中更可靠的伙伴。

Abstract: To maintain common ground in cooperative conversation, humans iteratively update their beliefs as conversation participants share new information; participants who are epistemically vigilant detect when new information conflicts with prior beliefs and take steps to repair these conflicts. In order for AI systems to serve as reliable partners in complex cooperative tasks, they must similarly weigh incoming information against their own private evidence and shared context and appropriately surface inconsistencies when they arise. To measure the epistemic vigilance of vision-language models in cooperative settings, we present an information-asymmetric, dialog-based "spot-the-difference" task. Two models are privately shown one image each, and must determine through conversation whether the images are identical or, if not, identify the difference. Models routinely fail at this: they frequently overlook key evidence in their private image in favor of agreeing with their conversational partner, even when their agreement is unwarranted. We relate these violations of epistemic vigilance to the broader behavior of sycophancy, which manifests itself in cooperative goal-oriented dialog as over-accommodation and weak evidential grounding. Our results show that model steering to reduce sycophancy with a vector learned from task-agnostic sycophancy examples can reduce epistemic vigilance-related errors, making models more faithful reporters of their evidence, and in turn, more reliable partners in information-asymmetric cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [123] [ResKV: Reconstructing Omitted Attention Contributions for Fixed-Budget KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29591)
*Yuhang Zhan,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ResKV提出一种KV缓存压缩方法，将固定预算分为精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存，残差缓存重建被淘汰token的注意力贡献，在相同预算下优于现有压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法中，驱逐法会永久丢弃token从而丢失其注意力聚合贡献，而合并法虽保留更多信息但会扰动本应保持精确的键值。作者观察到被驱逐token的注意力贡献可表示为softmax注意力分子分母中的残差统计量，从而提出用残差缓存来重建这些信息。

Method: ResKV将固定KV预算划分为精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存。主缓存token与残差条目参与同一个softmax归一化，使残差条目恢复注意力的分子和分母质量而非事后修正。构建时通过验证代理决定每层和每个KV头的残差分配，解码时通过动态门控调整每个查询的残差贡献。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER上进行全面评估，覆盖查询感知和查询无关设置、多种骨干网络、缓存预算和代表性压缩基线，在相同保留KV预算下展现广泛改进，同时保持压缩解码的实际效率，包括峰值内存和长上下文解码吞吐量。

Conclusion: ResKV通过残差缓存在不扰动主缓存精确性的前提下有效重建被驱逐token的注意力贡献，实现了性能与效率的更好权衡，为KV缓存压缩提供了新思路。

Abstract: KV cache compression is essential for efficient long-context inference. Existing eviction methods permanently discard unselected tokens and consequently remove their aggregate contribution to attention. Merging-based alternatives preserve more information but can perturb retained keys and values that should remain exact. We observe that the information omitted by cache eviction can be formulated as residual statistics in both the numerator and denominator of softmax attention. Based on this observation, we propose ResKV, which divides a fixed KV budget into an exact main cache and a compact residual cache that reconstructs the contribution of omitted tokens. ResKV lets main-cache tokens and residual entries participate in the same softmax normalization, so residual entries restore both attention numerator and denominator mass rather than acting as a post-hoc correction. A construction-time validation proxy determines residual allocation for each layer and KV head, while a decode-time dynamic gate adjusts residual contributions for individual queries. Comprehensive evaluations on LongBench and RULER, covering query-aware and query-agnostic settings, multiple backbones, cache budgets, and representative compression baselines, demonstrate broad improvements under the same retained KV budget while preserving the practical efficiency of compressed decoding, including peak memory usage and long-context decode throughput.

</details>


### [124] [FriendBench: Benchmarking Dyadic Familiarity Inference in Humans and Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29602)
*Jeffrey M. Girard,Jason Z. Zheng,Jacqueline R. Vertino,Antony D'Avirro,Benjamin Peloquin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FriendBench是一个基准测试，用于从20秒的双人破冰对话片段中推断两人是熟人还是陌生人。在96个平衡双人对话中，比较了来自7家公司的26个模型与人类小组在文本、音频和视频三种模态下的表现。最佳模型与人类在准确性上无统计差异，但人类在两个答案间保持平衡，而最强模型偏向"陌生人"——这是有效先验的差异而非判别能力差异。信息更丰富的通道对两者帮助不均，只有人类能从可见行为中获益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解社交情境往往依赖行为而非仅靠语言。现有模型在社交信号推断方面的能力缺乏系统评估，特别是判断两人是否相识这一基础社交任务尚未有专门的基准测试。

Method: 构建FriendBench基准：使用20秒双人破冰对话片段，所有对话对回答相同类型的提示，确保只有互动方式能揭示答案。在96个平衡双人对话上，对比26个模型（来自7家公司）与匹配的人类小组，分别在文本、音频、视频三种模态下进行评估。

Result: 最佳模型与人类在三种模态下准确性均无统计差异；人类在两种答案间保持平衡，而最强模型倾向于"陌生人"回答，差异在于有效先验而非判别能力；信息更丰富的通道对两者帮助程度不同，只有人类从可见行为中获益。

Conclusion: FriendBench揭示了模型在社交判断任务上能达到人类水平，但其决策偏差模式与人类不同。该基准与数据将公开，为后续社交智能研究提供测试基础。

Abstract: Reading a social situation often depends on behavior, not words alone. We introduce FriendBench, a benchmark for inferring whether two people are already familiar or are meeting as strangers, from a 20-second clip of a dyadic ice-breaker conversation. Every pair answers the same type of prompt, so only the manner of interaction can reveal the answer. Across text, audio, and video, we compare 26 models from seven companies against matched human panels over 96 balanced dyads. The best model and the human crowd are statistically indistinguishable on accuracy in every modality, but reach it differently: humans stay balanced across the two answers, while the strongest models lean toward "stranger"---a difference in effective prior, not discrimination. Richer channels help both unequally, and only humans gain from visible behavior on top of speech. We release the stimuli, human ratings, and model predictions.

</details>


### [125] [Evolving language compositionality in a frequency-structured meaning space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29642)
*Fabio De Ponte,Eloise Gaines-White,Conor Houghton,Seth Bullock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过迭代学习模型研究语言演化中频率分布对组合性（compositionality）的影响。发现高频含义可以逃脱语法压力，与自然语言中的观察一致；但当频率分布在更小的单元（而非完整含义向量）上时，语言无法跨代传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前研究已证明语言组合性可从重复传递和学习瓶颈中自发涌现，但未充分探讨含义频率分布如何影响组合性的特征。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究频率如何塑造涌现的语言结构。

Method: 使用迭代学习模型（iterated learning model），通过操纵含义的频率分布——将频率施加于完整含义向量（holistic meanings）或施加于部分/更小单元（parts rather than whole meaning vectors）——观察语言在跨代传播中的结构变化。

Result: 1）与自然语言一致，高频含义能够逃脱支配低频含义的语法压力；2）当频率分配在部分而非完整含义向量上时，即使高频元素被可靠学习，语言仍无法实现跨代传播。

Conclusion: 频率只有在定义于学习者能整体习得的形式-含义单元上时，才能塑造涌现的语言结构。当频率分布在更小单元上时，无法支持组合性泛化所需的关联结构，从而阻碍语言的稳定传播。

Abstract: The iterated learning model was introduced to investigate language evolution: the way in which the characteristic properties of human languages have been shaped, at least partly, by repeated transmission from one language user to another. The key finding is that language compositionality can arise spontaneously as a consequence of language being passed repeatedly through a language learning bottleneck. Here we explore how changing the frequency of different meanings, so that some meanings occur much more frequently than others, affects the character of its compositionality. We find that, as observed in natural languages, high-frequency meanings can escape the pressure to conform to the grammar that characterizes lower-frequency meanings. However, when the frequency structure is instead imposed on parts rather than on whole meaning vectors, the language fails to transmit across generations. This occurs despite the fact that the most frequent elements are reliably learned. These results suggest that frequency can shape emergent linguistic structure only when the frequency distribution is defined over form-meaning units that learners can acquire holistically. When frequency is instead distributed over smaller units, it fails to support the relational structure required for compositional generalisation, thereby preventing stable language transmission.

</details>


### [126] [TokTier: Exact Stateful Tokenization for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29678)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TokTier，一个状态化tokenization服务，通过增量窗口重tokenization拼接和GPU并行预处理，解决了LLM服务中重复tokenization的开销问题，在17种tokenizer系列、12.4TB真实文本上零分歧，显著降低首token延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务虽然缓存了prompt的KV状态，但大多数前端仍在每次调用时重新对整个请求文本进行tokenization。这对编码代理尤其昂贵，因为它们会频繁重新提交长对话记录。更关键的是，即使短小的追加内容也可能改变token边界，使复用变得困难。在94.1%的prompt缓存命中率下，tokenization可占用首token时间（TTFT）的64%，成为服务瓶颈。

Method: TokTier采用以下技术：1）对于会话延续，仅对追加位置附近的小窗口重新tokenize，并通过每请求的稳定边界检查验证后拼接，失败则扩大窗口或回退到完整tokenization；2）对于无复用前缀的调用，将GPT家族的正则预tokenization分解为局部规则，在GPU上执行精确预tokenization和BPE；3）通过采样的影子验证器实时检查流量，保证正确性。

Result: 在17种tokenizer系列上覆盖1.5×10^10次分割检查、12.4TB真实文本语料和9.3万+重放代理步骤，零分歧。增量修复仅需0.5-1.1ms（100K到3M字符），比HF快437倍，比最强缓存基线Gigatoken快2.1倍。GPU完整tokenization对1M字符请求仅需0.87ms。在vLLM下，中位TTFT降低16-34%，P99降低23%。在50ms P99目标下，4个修复核+1个GPU可支撑1,821请求/秒，而16核无状态前端仅饱和于40请求/秒。

Conclusion: TokTier证明了状态化tokenization可以在保持与参考tokenization完全一致的前提下，大幅降低LLM服务的tokenization开销，尤其在编码代理场景下效果显著，为LLM基础设施提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: LLM serving systems cache prompt KV state, yet most front ends still re-tokenize the full request text on every call. The cost lands on coding agents, which resubmit a long transcript after each small tool result, and reuse is hard because even a short append can change token boundaries near the end of the previous sequence. Across 153,951 calls from two agent ecosystems, the median call appends about 1.4K characters, and only 1.0-3.6% of calls start or rebuild a session with contexts of millions of characters. At a 94.1% fleet prompt-cache hit rate, tokenization reaches up to 64% of time to first token.
  TokTier is a stateful tokenization service with one contract: emitted token IDs are always identical to full reference tokenization of the request text. For a session continuation, it re-tokenizes a small window around the append and splices only after a per-request stable-boundary check, widening the window or falling back to full tokenization on failure. For a call without a reusable prefix, it decomposes GPT-family regex pre-tokenization into run-local rules and runs exact pre-tokenization and BPE on a GPU. A sampled shadow verifier re-checks live traffic.
  Across 17 tokenizer families, differential campaigns cover 1.5x10^10 split checks, a 12.4 TB real-text corpus, and 93,000+ replayed agent steps, with zero divergence. Incremental repair takes 0.5-1.1 ms from 100K to 3M characters, up to 437x faster than HF tokenization and 2.1x faster at 1M than the strongest cache-based baseline (Gigatoken) fully prewarmed. GPU full tokenization encodes a 1M-character request in 0.87 ms, up to 491x below HF and 23.4x below the fastest published CPU method. With vLLM, median time to first token drops 16-34% and P99 drops 23% under recorded bursts. Under a 50 ms P99 objective, four repair cores plus one GPU sustain 1,821 requests/s where a 16-core stateless front end saturates at 40.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Topology-Aware Data Movement for Disaggregated GPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28633)
*Sanjeev Rao Ganjihal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拓扑感知的KV缓存传输编排器，用于解决非聚合式LLM推理中prefill和decode分离导致的KV缓存跨GPU池传输问题。系统利用互联层次信息选择最优传输路径，通过流水线层间传输、NVLink域感知放置和CXL 3.0内存扩展三层机制，实现比均匀RDMA低3-18倍的传输延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非聚合式LLM推理系统（如DistServe、Splitwise、Mooncake）都使用均匀RDMA进行KV缓存传输，但完全忽略了数据中心内GPU间物理连接关系的巨大差异（带宽差异可达72倍）。对于70B模型，每次请求需要传输2.6GB的KV缓存，在规模生产时超过100GB/s的聚合带宽需求，现有方案无法正确处理这一问题。

Method: 设计了三个协同机制：(1) 流水线化的逐层传输，将传输与prefill计算重叠，可隐藏60-85%的延迟；(2) NVLink域感知放置策略，针对MoE模型同时优化专家分发和KV缓存局部性；(3) 利用CXL 3.0内存扩展器作为共享溢出层，提供6倍容量且延迟比NVMe低86倍。系统在启动时发现互联层级结构，为每次传输选择最优传输方式。

Result: 由于需要多节点异构互联集群和尚未在云GPU中可用的CXL 3.0硬件，无法进行完整评估。作者提供了分析性带宽模型、组件实现以及在三种架构上的预测分析，展示相对于均匀RDMA的3-18倍传输延迟降低。

Conclusion: 该工作指出了非聚合式LLM推理中一个被现有系统忽视的关键问题——拓扑感知传输的重要性，并提出了一个综合解决方案。虽然受限于硬件可用性无法完全验证，但分析结果预示了显著的性能提升潜力，为未来异构互联环境下的LLM推理系统设计提供了方向。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM inference creates a datacenter networking problem that no existing system solves correctly. When prefill and decode run on separate GPU pools, the KV cache must be transferred between them. For a 70B model this is 2.6 GB per request, exceeding 100 GB/s aggregate at production scale. Yet DistServe, Splitwise, and Mooncake all use uniform RDMA, ignoring that bandwidth between two GPUs varies by 72x depending on their physical relationship: 900 GB/s via NVLink within a domain, 50 GB/s via InfiniBand across nodes, 12.5 GB/s via TCP across data centers. We design a topology-aware transfer orchestrator that discovers interconnect hierarchy at startup and selects optimal transport per transfer. Three mechanisms work together: (1) pipelined layer-by-layer transfer that overlaps transmission with ongoing prefill, hiding 60 to 85 percent of latency behind computation; (2) NVLink domain-aware placement for Mixture-of-Experts models that co-optimizes expert dispatch with KV cache locality; and (3) CXL 3.0 memory expanders as a shared overflow tier providing 6x capacity at 86x lower latency than NVMe. Full evaluation requires multi-node clusters with heterogeneous interconnects and CXL 3.0 hardware that is beyond academic resources and not yet available in GPU clouds. We present analytical bandwidth models, component implementations, and projected analysis across three architectures showing 3 to 18x transfer latency reduction over uniform RDMA.

</details>


### [128] [Sensitivity Analysis of GRU, LSTM and Transformer Encoder in Classification of Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28665)
*Bidhya Shrestha,Christos Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何仅利用车辆遥测数据来识别不同的Level 2自动驾驶系统（Comma Openpilot、Tesla Autopilot、Cadillac Super Cruise及人工驾驶），比较了GRU、LSTM和Transformer三种序列分类模型的性能，并引入了一个模块化的鲁棒性评估框架来模拟五种真实世界数据退化类型。结果表明三种模型在干净数据上表现优秀（宏F1达0.90-0.93），但时间抖动会导致性能急剧下降至0.44-0.50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶系统日益普及，未来软件定义车辆可能同时运行多个自动驾驶系统（原生和售后市场）。独立监控系统以验证哪个自动驾驶系统处于激活状态，对于安全监控、法规合规、保险评估和异常检测至关重要。

Method: 首先评估三种序列分类模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer编码器）对自动驾驶系统的识别能力；其次引入模块化鲁棒性评估框架，模拟五种腐败类型（加性高斯白噪声加累积漂移、相关跨通道噪声、时间抖动、事件级突发丢失/延迟转换/虚假切换/跨特征不一致）在五个严重级别（L1-L5）下的退化情况，使用宏F1分数作为评估指标。

Result: 三种模型在干净数据上表现优秀（宏F1分数：GRU 0.92、LSTM 0.90、Transformer 0.93）；威胁匹配训练后宏F1为0.904-0.916，仅有轻微的干净数据性能损失。鲁棒性评估显示事件级腐败对性能影响较小（L5时宏F1仍≥0.87），但时间抖动使宏F1骤降至0.44-0.50。

Conclusion: 序列模型能够有效识别自动驾驶系统，但时间抖动是关键的鲁棒性弱点，需要在未来的系统设计中重点解决。

Abstract: Automated driving systems (ADSs) are becoming ubiquitous. Future Software Defined Vehicles (SDVs) may be able to run multiple ADSs, both native and aftermarket such as Comma.ai's Openpilot. Monitoring systems to independently verify which automated driving system is active are important for safety monitoring, regulatory compliance, insurance assessment, and anomaly detection. In this paper, we first evaluate the effectiveness of three sequence-based classification models: Gated Recurrent Units (GRU), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and a Transformer encoder model for identifying Level 2 automated driving systems using vehicle telematics data alone: Comma Openpilot, Tesla Autopilot, and Cadillac Super Cruise, along with manual driving. All three models achieve strong clean-data performance with macro F1-scores of 0.92 (GRU), 0.90 (LSTM), and 0.93 (Transformer encoder model) when trained on clean data; threat-matched training yields 0.904-0.916 macro F1 with only a modest clean-data penalty. Second, we introduce a modular robustness evaluation framework that simulates realistic telematics degradation through five corruption families at five severity levels (L1-L5). Continuous channels are perturbed using additive white Gaussian noise with cumulative drift, correlated cross-channel noise, and temporal jitter. Binary event signals are subjected to burst loss, delayed transitions, spurious toggles and cross-feature inconsistencies inspired by communication errors. Robustness is measured using macro-F1, which gives equal weight to each class and is suitable for imbalanced multiclass evaluation. Our evaluation reveals a sharp failure-mode split: event-level corruptions reduce macro-F1 only slightly (greater than equal to 0.87 at L5), while temporal jitter collapses macro-F1 to 0.44-0.50 across GRU, LSTM, and Transformer encoder model.

</details>


### [129] [Autonomous Repair for Multi-Agent Systems via Monte-Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29055)
*Hanxiao Lu,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARS框架，将多智能体系统（MAS）的修复问题形式化为蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）过程，通过诊断引导的扩展和分类法增强的评估来导航庞大的修复空间。同时引入StateMAS基准测试，包含1,310条可重放的多智能体失败轨迹。实验表明MARS在所有设置中绝对提升3.0%至12.1%，且token消耗相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在输出不正确时，用户需要手动定位智能体错误（失败归因）并提供反馈进行修复，但现有的自动化修复机制尚缺乏探索。作者希望填补这一空白，实现从失败归因到自动修复的跨越。

Method: 1) 将MAS修复形式化为MCTS搜索过程；2) 提出诊断引导的扩展策略来导航修复空间；3) 使用分类法增强的评估方法；4) 采用部分rollout（而非完整rollout）来评估智能体轨迹，降低token消耗；5) 构建StateMAS基准，覆盖4种智能体架构和4种LLM骨干。

Result: MARS在所有设置中一致优于最先进方法，绝对提升3.0%到12.1%，同时token消耗成本相当。消融实验证实分类法增强的评估和诊断引导的扩展是实现性能提升的关键组件。

Conclusion: MARS成功实现了多智能体系统的自动化修复，填补了该领域的研究空白。诊断引导扩展与分类法增强评估被证明是核心贡献，部分rollout策略在保持性能的同时有效控制了计算成本。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly deployed to solve complex tasks. In case of incorrect or unsatisfactory outputs, users have to manually locate agent mistakes by inspecting agent trajectories (i.e., {\em failure attribution}) and provide feedback to refine the outputs (i.e., {\em repair}). Despite some recent work in MAS failure attribution, automated mechanisms to recover from such mistakes remain largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose MARS, a search-based framework that formulates MAS repair as a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) process and navigates the vast space of potential repairs via diagnosis-guided expansion with taxonomy-augmented evaluation. Unlike standard MCTS, which evaluates a complete simulation via full rollout, MARS evaluates the agent trajectory using partial rollout to reduce token consumption. Furthermore, we introduce StateMAS, a large-scale MAS repair benchmark with 1,310 replayable multi-agent failure trajectories spanning four types of agent architectures and four LLM backbones. Experiments on StateMAS demonstrate that MARS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving an absolute improvement from 3.0\% to 12.1\% across all settings, while maintaining a comparable token consumption cost. The ablation study further confirms that taxonomy-augmented evaluation and diagnosis-guided expansion are critical to achieving these performance gains.

</details>


### [130] [Guarantees on Dynamical System Distinguishability for LLM Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28667)
*Mohamed Akrout,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于动力系统（DS）的LLM响应分类方法进行了理论分析，将其形式化为两个随机线性DS之间的二元假设检验，并证明了分类误差随序列长度呈指数衰减，同时建立了跨嵌入泛化的理论界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于动力系统的LLM分类方法在经验上取得了成功，但其理论基础尚不明确：为什么有效、如何随序列长度扩展、以及何时能跨嵌入模型迁移。作者希望填补这一理论空白，为该方法提供严格的数学解释。

Method: 将分类任务形式化为两个随机线性动力系统之间的二元假设检验。推导了两个DS平稳边际分布之间的总变差距离的下界（可任意小），证明忽略token动力学的分类器存在准确率下限；再证明基于DS的分类误分类概率随序列长度L呈指数衰减，衰减速率由动力学判别量δ²（反映两DS的谱距离）决定；最后通过引入嵌入模型间的近似交织条件（approximate intertwining condition），建立跨嵌入可迁移判别性的下界（依赖于交织映射的最小奇异值）。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 仅依赖平稳边际分布的忽略动力学的分类器存在根本性准确率下限；(2) DS分类的误分类概率随序列长度指数衰减，由谱距离δ²控制；(3) 跨嵌入迁移的可判别性可由交织映射的最小奇异值下界刻画。

Conclusion: 这些理论结果解释了DS分类方法的经验性能，并为利用DS理论分析AI系统（而非用AI建模动力系统）这一方向提供了理论动机和支持。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that classifying large language models (LLMs)' responses can be distinguished by modeling token embeddings as trajectories of a black-box dynamical system (DS) and comparing prediction residuals of two DSs. Despite the empirical success of this dynamical approach, a theoretical understanding of why it works, how well it scales as a function of the token sequence, and when it transfers across embedding models remains lacking. We address these questions by formalizing the classification task as a binary hypothesis test between two stochastic linear DSs. We show that the total variation distance between the stationary marginal distributions of the two DSs can be arbitrarily small even when the dynamics differ substantially, which provides a fundamental accuracy floor for any classifier that ignores token dynamics. We then show that the misclassification probability of DS-based classification decays exponentially in the sequence length $L$, with the decay governed by a dynamical discriminability quantity $δ^2$ that captures the spectral distance between the two DSs. We also characterize cross-embedding generalization by introducing an approximate intertwining condition between embedding models and establishing a lower bound on the transferable discriminability in terms of the intertwining map's smallest singular value. Together, these results explain the empirical performance of DS-based classification and motivate further investigation into using DS theory to analyze AI systems, in contrast to the more common approach of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [131] [LARA: Lightweight Adapters in the Residual Stream for Composable Adaptation and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28669)
*Pascal Ekin,Hyosun Choi,Wei Jie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LARA是一种轻量级加法残差适配方法，在冻结模型的残差流（而非权重）中进行高效适配。它在少量层读取隐藏状态并将低秩修正加回残差流，保持所有基础权重不变。在代码微调和偏好优化（DPO）任务上与LoRA在同等参数量的条件下表现相当，并提供了推理时可调的缩放系数γ实现基线与适配行为间的平滑插值，还支持多个行为同时驻留并按token自动路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调（PEFT）方法如LoRA在权重空间中注入低秩更新，但存在两大局限：一是无法在推理时对适配程度进行细粒度调节（权重空间适配不可插值）；二是多个适配行为难以同时驻留于单个模型，需按需加载，不利于多行为托管。作者希望探索一种在残差流中进行适配的新范式，以克服上述限制。

Method: LARA在冻结模型的一小组层中读取残差流的隐藏状态，然后添加一个低秩修正并写回残差流，所有基础权重保持不变。推理时引入标量γ对适配残差进行缩放，实现从基础行为到适配行为的平滑插值。由于每个行为是一个叠加在共享冻结基座上的小型残差模块，多个行为可同时驻留，并按token粒度自动选择路由。

Result: 在代码微调任务和DPO偏好优化上，LARA在同等参数数量下达成了与LoRA相当的性能。作者在单个冻结的1.5B模型上同时部署了七个行为（六个微调行为加一个偏好优化行为），总体开销仅约33MB，而传统方案则需为每个行为单独维护一个完整模型。由于基础权重不变，各行为可独立训练并按token选择性路由，无需按需加载，非常适合在单设备单模型上托管多个行为及持续添加新行为。

Conclusion: LARA证明了在残差流中进行适配是一种可行且高效的新范式，在性能上与LoRA持平的同时，额外提供了权重空间适配所不具备的插值控制能力和多行为共存能力，为参数高效微调开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: We present LARA (Lightweight Additive Residual Adaptation), a method for efficient adaptation that operates in the residual stream of a frozen model rather than in its weights. Where LoRA adds an update of low rank to weight matrices, LARA reads the hidden state at a small set of layers and adds a correction of low rank back to the residual stream, leaving all base weights untouched. On a code fine-tuning task and on preference optimization (DPO), LARA matches LoRA at equal parameter counts. Because adaptation is a frozen base plus a residual, LARA exposes a scale γ, applied at inference, that interpolates smoothly between base and adapted behavior, a form of graded control that adaptation in weight space does not offer. Finally, because each behavior is a small residual module over a shared frozen base, many behaviors can be held resident at once and routed automatically per token. We place seven behaviors, six fine-tuned and one optimized for preference, on one frozen 1.5B model for roughly 33 MB of overhead, against one full model for each behavior. Because the base is untouched, behaviors are trained separately and selected per token rather than loaded on demand, which suits hosting many behaviors, and adding new ones, on a single model on a device.

</details>


### [132] [Transcript-Managed Transformers: Monotone Multi-Agent Collapse and Universality with Two Pop-Enabled Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29496)
*Sergey Salishev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究固定有限精度因果Transformer的转录管理（transcript management）。转录被划分为有界块的信道，每次转换仅查看固定的可见后缀并可追加一个块。模型为转录管理换能器TMTn{k}：一个有限控制器、k个信道，每轮执行stay/push/pop操作。不带pop的受限版本RTMTn{k}恰好实现确定性有限状态换能，而启用pop后，单信道等价于下推自动机（DCFL），两个及以上信道可达图灵完备（RE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer等因果序列模型在有限精度和固定可见窗口下的计算能力边界，特别是转录管理与经典自动机层级（DCFL、RE）之间的关系。

Method: 引入转录管理换能器TMTn{k}形式化模型，将固定可见窗口编码为有限符号，通过pop上下文操作P_c恢复栈语义；将k=1映射到Hopcroft-Ullman下推自动机，k≥2映射到通用图灵机；分析单智能体与多智能体（有限种群）的等价性。

Result: RTMTn{k}（无pop，纯追加）对所有固定k恰好实现确定性有限状态换能。启用pop后：k=1精确实现DCFL（确定性上下文无关语言），k≥2实现RE（递归可枚举语言/图灵完备）。一个双信道智能体或两个单信道智能体即可达到通用性。文章还给出了模拟代价、对固定块大小和可见半径的不变性结论。

Conclusion: 转录管理模型将Transformer的固定有限精度因果计算与经典自动机理论层级精确对应：纯追加等价于有限状态机，启用pop后单栈为DCFL，双栈即图灵完备。有限的精度、字母表、块大小、可见性、控制器状态和种群规模决定了该层级，而增长上下文、隐藏块访问、可写存储和无限生成（Spawn）会进一步提升表达能力。

Abstract: We study transcript management for fixed, finite-precision causal Transformers. A transcript is partitioned into channels of bounded blocks. Each transition consults a fixed visible suffix and may append one block, leaving the model, weights, and token protocol unchanged. The operation $P_c:=\PopContext(c)$ deletes the newest block on channel $c$ and exposes its predecessor.
  We model the layer by the Transcript-Managed Transducer $\TMTn{k}$: one finite controller, $k$ channels, and per-round actions from stay, push, and pop under a caller-driven status map. Fixed visible windows encode as finite symbols. The pop-free Restricted Transcript-Managed Transducer $\RTMTn{k}$ is the standard append-only layer and, for every fixed $k$, realizes exactly the deterministic finite-state transductions. The same holds for every fixed finite agent population under a monotone protocol that appends, routes, and copies visible blocks.
  Admitting $\{P_c\}_{c=1}^k$ restores pop. Newest-first, a pop-enabled channel is a stack; compiling to the Hopcroft--Ullman presentation transfers the classical hierarchy: $\DCFL$ for $k=1$ and $\RE$ for every $k\ge2$. Orchestrated one-channel agents match one controller with $k$ channels, so two pop-enabled transcripts---in one agent or two---suffice for universality. Simulation costs and invariance to fixed block size and visible radius are stated. The bounds fix precision, alphabets, blocks, visibility, controller state, and population; growing exact context, hidden-block access, writable stores, and unbounded \textbf{Spawn} add further state.

</details>


### [133] [Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-\texorpdfstring{$K$}{K} Routing: Two-Sided Dependence Control for Frozen Mixture-of-Experts at Fixed Per-Token Routing Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28670)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-K (CGA) 路由机制，通过高斯copula正相关耦合组内token的Gumbel扰动、反演构造引入组间负相关，在保持每个token路由分布完全不变的前提下，调控MoE中专家负载的相干性与离散度。理论证明两种操作在条件于预路由logits下不改变有序Top-K样本、混合权重及包含概率的分布，并刻画了正耦合抬升负载方差、负相关降低离散度的权衡。基于冻结特征的小型控制器可用score-function估计器训练，初步实验验证了机制但未证明任务级增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型中标准Gumbel-Top-K路由假设各token的扰动独立，导致同组相关token（如同一句子的token）可能被分散到不同专家，破坏专家分配的语义连贯性；同时独立路由会产生不可控的负载波动。现有方法无法在不改变单个token路由分布的前提下调控token间的联合路由依赖关系，本论文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-K (CGA) 双层构建：(1) 组内层：用可交换高斯copula对组内token的Gumbel扰动在各专家坐标上施加正相关，增强组内专家集一致性；(2) 组间层：对不相交组对引入可调的反演（antithetic）构造，产生可选择的负相关。论文证明了这两种操作在条件于预路由logits的层面，不改变每个token的有序Top-K分布、混合权重及包含概率；同时刻画了负载方差权衡：组内正耦合抬高负载方差，组间非负反对降低方差。控制器基于冻结特征，通过score-function估计器训练。

Result: 论文给出了CGA构建的完整理论刻画，证明其保持每个token的路由分布精确不变（条件于预路由logits），同时以互补的依赖旋钮分别控制相干性和负载离散度。通过初步小规模pilot实验验证了机制可行性和训练路径，但并未建立任务级微调的增益证据。

Conclusion: 本论文建立了一个在分布不变约束曲面上调控MoE路由联合依赖性的理论框架，将相干性与负载分散性解耦为两个互补的控制维度，并为基于冻结特征的轻量控制器提供了可训练方案。不过任务级收益仍需后续更大规模的实证验证。

Abstract: A stochastic Gumbel-Top-$K$ router defines, for every token of a mixture-of-experts (MoE) model, a \emph{routing law}: a distribution over ordered expert lists and mixture weights. We ask which \emph{joint} distributions over the routing choices of different tokens are reachable while every individual token's complete routing law is held exactly fixed. We give a two-sided construction, \emph{Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-$K$} (\CGA{}). Within a group of related tokens, an exchangeable Gaussian copula positively correlates the Gumbel perturbations at each expert coordinate, which can increase within-group expert-set coherence. Across disjoint pairs of groups, a tunable antithetic construction introduces a selectable amount of negative dependence. We prove that both operations leave each token's ordered Top-$K$ sample, mixture weights, and inclusion probabilities identical in distribution to independent routing \emph{at a routing layer conditioned on its pre-routing logits}; conditional expected expert traffic is preserved as a consequence. We characterize the resulting trade-off: positive within-group coupling can only inflate the variance of realized expert loads relative to independent routing, while nonnegative cross-group opposition can only reduce it relative to flat coupling at the same within-group strength. Coherence and load dispersion are thus controlled by two complementary dependence dials on the invariance constraint surface. Because the base model is untouched, the dials can be driven by a small controller over frozen features, trainable with a score-function estimator: the frozen network is evaluated only in the forward direction, and gradients are confined to the controller. An initial small-scale pilot validates the mechanism and the training route, but does not establish task-level fine-tuning gains.

</details>


### [134] [LAWFUL: Law-Aligned Witness for Faithful Use of Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28672)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LAWFUL框架，用于弥合神经网络在连续物理定律可解释性中的四个关键缺口：覆盖感知的因果一致性度量、电路的有效域测试、定律不变量与禁止行为验证，以及物理量在电路中的流动量化。作者在Mocap2Radar transformer上验证了该框架，检验网络是否真正学习并在内部使用多普勒频率定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当神经网络准确预测物理系统时，一个根本问题随之而来：网络是否真正学习并内部使用了形式化的物理定律？现有可解释性方法在连续变量物理定律上存在四个缺口：缺乏覆盖感知的连续反事实因果一致性度量、缺乏识别电路的有效域测试、缺乏对定律不变量和禁止行为的验证、缺乏物理量通过电路的流动量化。

Method: 提出了LAWFUL框架，这是一个基础性框架，闭环了前两个缺口（覆盖感知的因果一致性度量、电路有效域测试），并为后两个缺口（不变量/禁止行为验证、物理量流动量化）奠定基础。该框架在Mocap2Radar transformer上进行验证，该模型从运动捕捉和雷达数据中学习多普勒频率定律，且输入中不直接包含f(t)或v(t)。

Result: LAWFUL框架成功应用于Mocap2Radar transformer，验证了网络是否学习并在内部使用多普勒频率定律f(t)=2v(t)/λ，即在训练数据中两个物理量均未显式出现的情况下，检验网络是否隐含地捕获了这一物理关系。

Conclusion: LAWFUL为连续物理定律的神经网络可解释性提供了系统化基础，部分弥合了四个关键缺口，为更深入地理解神经网络是否真正“理解”物理定律及其内部计算机制铺平了道路。

Abstract: When a neural network predicts a physical system accurately, has it learned the governing law as formal, structured knowledge, and if so, does the network's internal computation actually use that representation throughout the law's domain of validity? We identify four interpretability gaps that limit answering these questions for {\em physics laws over continuous variables}: the absence of a coverage-aware causal-consistency measure over continuous counterfactuals; of a domain-of-validity test for the identified circuit; of a verification of the law's invariants and forbidden behaviors; and of a quantification of how a derived physical quantity flows through the circuit. We develop a foundational framework, LAWFUL, that closes the first two and lays groundwork for the remaining two, and illustrate it on the Mocap2Radar transformer, validating whether it learns and internally uses the Doppler frequency law $f(t) = \frac{2 v(t)}λ$ from motion-capture and radar data in which neither $f(t)$ nor $v(t)$ appears.

</details>


### [135] [MPP-GNN: Subject-Adaptive Community Detection for fMRI-Based Alzheimer's Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28681)
*Yang Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Jonathan Warrell,Avram Holmes,Xuan Zhang,Mark Gerstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种元概率池化图神经网络（MPP-GNN），用于fMRI脑功能连接分析中的阿尔茨海默病（AD）分类。该方法通过耦合双层优化实现自适应层次图分区，发现个体特异性的脑功能模块，并将这些模块作为显式先验指导边细化和表征学习，在两个公开数据集上取得了最高的AUC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的脑功能连接分析方法通常预设所有受试者共享相同数量的功能模块，忽略了受试者间的变异性；此外，发现的功能模块很少被直接用于指导学习到的连接模式。这两个问题限制了模型性能和可解释性。

Method: MPP-GNN将任务建模为耦合的双层优化问题：1）通过自适应层次图分区发现个体特异性的脑功能模块；2）将发现的大脑模块作为显式先验，指导边细化和表征学习过程。

Result: 在两个公共AD分类数据集上，MPP-GNN相比已有基线方法取得了最高的AUC。分析还表明该方法与Yeo脑图谱定义的标准功能网络组织有显著对齐，并揭示了AD的神经网络级去分化模式。

Conclusion: MPP-GNN通过个体自适应的模块发现与模块指导的学习框架，不仅提升了AD分类性能，还增强了模型的可解释性，能够揭示与疾病相关的功能网络重组模式。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a widely used technique for studying the brain. Recent methods that utilize graph neural networks (GNNs) for analysis of brain functional connectivity have shown great potential for the classification of brain disorders, such as Alzheimer's disease (AD). However, these methods often assume a preset number of functional modules across all subjects, which overlooks inter-subject variability. In addition, the discovered modules are rarely used to directly guide the learned connectivity patterns. Here, to address these issues, we propose a Meta Probabilistic Pooling GNN (MPP-GNN). We frame the model's task as a coupled, bilevel optimization that performs adaptive graph partitioning hierarchically to discover subject-specific modules and then uses the discovered brain modules as an explicit prior to guide edge refinement and representation learning. We validate MPP-GNN on two public datasets for AD classification, achieving the highest AUC in comparison to established baselines for both datasets. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that MPP-GNN shows significant alignment with the canonical functional-network organization defined by the Yeo brain atlas and reveals a network-level dedifferentiation pattern for AD.

</details>


### [136] [Technological Advances in Detecting and Managing Cognitive Impairment in Older Adults: Trends, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28687)
*Mohammad Asif,Azizuddin Khan,Mohd Azam,Anurag Rajkumar Bombarde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判性地综述了用于老年人认知障碍（从轻度认知障碍MCI到痴呆）检测与管理的最新科技进展，涵盖EEG神经生理信号、MRI/淀粉样蛋白/tau PET神经影像、血液生物标志物和数字标记，并通过AI/ML/DL技术进行整合。文章提出了跨学科分类法、方法学严谨性透镜、分层筛查到干预的整合早期检测框架，以及检测方法、干预措施和风险/保护因素的比较表格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人口老龄化，从轻度认知障碍到痴呆的认知衰退将成为未来几十年的关键健康挑战，但常规评估常常错过其最早期的迹象，亟需更先进、更可靠的检测和管理方法。

Method: 本文采用批判性综合方法，系统梳理了多模态检测技术（EEG、神经影像、血液生物标志物、数字标记）及其AI/ML/DL整合策略，提出跨学科分类法、强调主体与站点独立验证的方法学严格性标准，并构建了将分层筛查与干预相连接的整合早期检测框架及多维度比较表格。

Result: EEG标志物（alpha/theta变化、P300潜伏期）和深度模型（CNN、LSTM/BiLSTM、transformer、自监督EEG基础模型）报告了高准确率，但许多基于小型单站点数据集，难以通过严格外部验证。血浆p-tau217已具备临床实用性，首个血检在2025年获准辅助阿尔茨海默病诊断；抗淀粉样蛋白疗法（lecanemab、donanemab）已获批但获益有争议；多领域生活方式预防已成熟。可穿戴、远程、语音和VR工具实现了持续生态效度监测，多模态融合提升了敏感性和特异性。

Conclusion: 该领域的近期前景在于构建可信、多模态、纵向验证的系统，将早期检测与可操作的个性化护理相连接。仍面临标准化、可解释性、数据隐私和公平的外部验证部署等障碍。

Abstract: As populations age, cognitive decline from mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to dementia is a defining health challenge of the coming decades, yet routine assessment often misses its earliest signs. This article critically synthesizes recent technological advances for detecting and managing cognitive impairment in older adults, spanning neurophysiological signals (chiefly electroencephalography, EEG), structural and molecular neuroimaging (MRI and amyloid/tau PET), blood-based biomarkers, and digital markers, integrated through artificial intelligence (AI), machine learning (ML), and deep learning (DL). Beyond summarizing, it contributes a cross-disciplinary taxonomy, a methodological-rigor lens foregrounding subject- and site-independent validation, an integrative early-detection framework linking tiered screening to intervention, and comparison tables of detection methods, interventions, and risk and protective factors. EEG markers (alpha/theta changes, P300 latency) and deep models (CNNs, LSTM/BiLSTM, transformers, self-supervised EEG foundation models) report strong accuracy, yet many rest on small, single-site datasets unlikely to survive rigorous external validation. Elsewhere, gains are tangible: plasma p-tau217 has reached clinical utility, with the first blood test cleared to aid Alzheimer's diagnosis in 2025; anti-amyloid therapies (lecanemab, donanemab) are approved despite modest, contested benefits; and multidomain lifestyle prevention has matured. Wearable, remote, speech, and virtual-reality tools enable continuous, ecologically valid monitoring, and multimodal fusion improves sensitivity and specificity. Barriers remain: standardization, explainability, data privacy, and equitable, externally validated deployment. The field's near-term promise lies in trustworthy, multimodal, longitudinally validated systems linking early detection to actionable, personalized care.

</details>


### [137] [SEDR-Seq2P: A Lightweight Dilated Residual Sequence-to-Point Network for Multi-Task Industrial NILM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28693)
*Hatem Haddad,Feres Jerbi,Issam Smaali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对工业NILM场景下的挑战（测量噪声和多设备并发运行导致模型泛化差），提出SEDR-Seq2P轻量级Seq2Point扩展模型，通过膨胀残差块和挤压激励注意力机制，在IMDELD基准上实现了比Seq2Point基线MAE降低约7%、比WaveNet推理延迟降低约58%的精度-延迟平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业NILM面临测量噪声和多台机器并发运行的问题，基于住宅数据调优的模型泛化能力差。现有模型如Seq2Seq、Seq2SubSeq、Seq2Point、GRU和WaveNet在精度与计算成本之间存在明显的权衡矛盾，需要一种既保持高精度又具有低推理延迟的轻量级方案。

Method: 采用一对多（one-to-many）多任务分解设定，单一网络从总功率中估计多个工业设备负载。提出SEDR-Seq2P模型，是基于Seq2Point的轻量级扩展，包含两个关键组件：膨胀残差块（dilated residual blocks）以扩大感受野，以及挤压激励注意力（squeeze-and-excitation attention）以增强特征选择能力。

Result: 在IMDELD数据集上，与Seq2Point基线相比，SEDR-Seq2P将MAE降低约7%，决定系数提高约1%，匹配率提高约0.8%。与WaveNet相比，推理延迟降低约58%。GRU和WaveNet虽然精度更高，但计算成本显著更高；Seq2Point在精度-延迟平衡上优于Seq2Seq/Seq2SubSeq。

Conclusion: SEDR-Seq2P在精度与延迟之间取得了有利的权衡，适用于可扩展的工业部署场景，为工业NILM提供了一种轻量高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial NILM remains challenging because measurement noise and widespread concurrent machine operation reduce the generalization of models tuned on residential data. This work adopts a one-to-many, multi-task disaggregation setting, in which a single network estimates multiple industrial machine loads from aggregate power. Under a unified evaluation protocol on IMDELD, we benchmark Seq2Seq, Seq2SubSeq, Seq2Point, GRU, and WaveNet using energy-estimation metrics and the accuracy-delay criterion. While Seq2Point offers a stronger accuracy-delay balance than Seq2Seq/Seq2SubSeq, GRU and WaveNet achieve higher accuracy at markedly higher computational cost. To close this gap, we propose SEDR-Seq2P, a lightweight Seq2Point extension with dilated residual blocks and squeeze-and-excitation attention. Relative to the Seq2Point baseline, SEDR-Seq2P reduces MAE by approximately 7%, improves the coefficient of determination by approximately 1%, and increases the match rate by approximately 0.8%. In addition, compared to WaveNet, SEDR-Seq2P reduces inference latency by approximately 58%, yielding a favorable accuracy-delay trade-off for scalable industrial deployment.

</details>


### [138] [Predicting Steel Fatigue Life from Micrographs Using Physics-Informed Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28695)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CV框架，直接从光学显微图像预测轻质合金钢的疲劳寿命（log Nf），无需物理测试。通过七阶段OpenCV预处理、28维物理信息特征提取和CNN回归模型（采用GNNL损失），在合成数据集上ResNet-50达到R²=0.93，并将校准误差降低76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构钢疲劳寿命评估需要数十至数百小时的力学测试，无法用于快速质量控制。亟需一种无需物理测试、从微观结构直接快速估算疲劳寿命的方法。

Method: 包含三个核心部分：1) 七阶段OpenCV预处理管道用于去除伪影；2) 28维物理信息特征提取器（量化裂纹形态、晶粒结构、孔隙率和纹理）；3) CNN回归模型，使用高斯负对数似然损失联合预测log Nf和样本特定不确定性。评估了SE-CNN、ResNet-50、VGG-16三种架构。

Result: ResNet-50在合成显微图基准上实现R²=0.93、RMSE=0.18 log周期、macro-F1=0.91。GNLL目标相比MSE基线将期望校准误差降低76%（ECE：0.089→0.021）。每张图像处理时间小于65ms。Grad-CAM确认网络关注冶金学上有意义的显微结构特征。

Conclusion: 该方法在模拟条件下验证了方法论的正确性，但当前验证完全依赖合成显微图；在真实现场样本上的域迁移研究是下一步工作。管线和合成数据集生成器已开源。

Abstract: Here is the plain text version optimized for arXiv's submission form. Custom macros (like \CV and \SI) have been converted to standard text/math so they render correctly on the webpage: Evaluating the fatigue life of structural steels conventionally requires mechanical testing lasting tens to hundreds of hours, making it impractical for rapid quality control. We present CV, a computer vision framework that estimates the fatigue life ($\log N_f$) of lightweight alloy steels directly from optical micrographs without physical testing.The pipeline features a seven-stage OpenCV preprocessing routine to remove artifacts, a 28-dimensional physics-informed feature extractor (quantifying crack morphology, grain structure, porosity, and texture), and a CNN regression model trained with a Gaussian negative log-likelihood (GNLL) loss to jointly predict $\log N_f$ and sample-specific uncertainty $\hatσ$.Evaluating three architectures (SE-CNN, ResNet-50, VGG-16) on a synthetic micrograph benchmark, ResNet-50 achieves $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE = 0.18 log-cycles, and macro-F1 = 0.91. The GNLL objective reduces Expected Calibration Error by 76% compared to a mean-squared-error baseline (ECE: $0.089 \rightarrow 0.021$). Grad-CAM maps confirm the network attends to metallurgically meaningful microstructural features.Running in under 65 ms per image, the pipeline and synthetic dataset generator are open-sourced. Because validation relies entirely on synthetic micrographs, these results demonstrate methodological soundness under simulated conditions; a domain-transfer study on real field samples is the immediate next step.

</details>


### [139] [Mitigating Class-Tail Undercoverage in Medical Vision-Language Models under Clinical Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28696)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CALCoDe，一种针对医学视觉-语言模型（VLM）的后验可靠性层，用于解决临床分布偏移下某些疾病类别覆盖不足的问题。它结合局部化（conformal）阈值与类别条件尾部阈值，在保证有限样本覆盖的同时，对偏移外部队列保持经验上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VLM在临床分布偏移后虽能维持总体边际覆盖，但单个疾病类别的覆盖可能严重不足，且受采集协议和骨干网络影响，源域疾病流行度无法可靠预测失败类别。现有的局部化和尾部感知conformal方法分别适应测试邻域和源频率尾部，未能建模这种留出的类别级覆盖失败。

Method: CALCoDe包含三步：(1) 交叉拟合验证预测识别风险类别；(2) 不相交的校准集估计这些类别的类别条件尾部阈值；(3) 通过单侧最大值将受保护阈值与局部化conformal阈值结合，使结果集包含局部化规则接纳的所有标签。另设独立校准的支持审计用于延迟支持不足的样本。

Result: 在两种皮肤科偏移（HAM10000到ISIC 2019和HAM10000到PAD-UFES-20）及四种冻结VLM骨干（BiomedCLIP、CLIP ViT-B/32、PubMedCLIP、MedSigLIP-448）共八种设置中，CALCoDe是唯一使边际和最差类接受覆盖均达到0.95的方法。在HAM10000到ISIC 2019上，平均最差类接受覆盖为0.970，优于sTACP（0.926）和LCP-VLM（0.864）。

Conclusion: CALCoDe能够在保持局部化conformal集合包含性的同时，为验证识别的风险类别提供受保护的有限样本覆盖保证，并在经验上对偏移外部队列表现鲁棒，为医学VLM提供了一种类感知的后验可靠性增强方案。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) can retain high observed marginal coverage after clinical shift while substantially under-covering an individual disease class. The affected class varies with acquisition protocol and backbone geometry, so source prevalence does not reliably reveal the failure. Existing localized and tail-aware conformal methods respectively adapt to test neighborhoods and source-frequency tails, leaving held-out class-wise coverage failure unmodeled. We introduce Class-Tail Adaptive Localized Conformal Deferral (CALCoDe), a post-hoc reliability layer for frozen medical VLMs. Cross-fitted validation predictions identify classes at risk of undercoverage, and a disjoint calibration split estimates their class-conditional tail thresholds. CALCoDe combines each protected threshold with a localized conformal threshold using a one-sided maximum. The resulting set contains every label admitted by the localized rule, with additional protection confined to validation-identified classes. An independently calibrated support audit defers cases with insufficient inlier support. Under exchangeability among accepted examples within each protected class, CALCoDe provides finite-sample coverage at the prespecified guard level and contains the corresponding localized conformal sets; coverage on shifted external cohorts is evaluated empirically. Among standard conformal baselines and recent VLM-specific conformal methods evaluated across two dermatology shifts (HAM10000 to ISIC 2019 and HAM10000 to PAD-UFES-20) and four frozen VLM backbones (BiomedCLIP, OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32, PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32, and MedSigLIP-448), CALCoDe is the only approach whose observed marginal and worst-class accepted coverage both reach 0.95 in all eight settings. On HAM10000 to ISIC 2019, its average worst-class accepted coverage is 0.970, compared with 0.926 for sTACP and 0.864 for LCP-VLM.

</details>


### [140] [Flow Matching with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28698)
*Fairoz Nower Khan,Nabuat Zaman Nahim,Peizhong Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Missing-Data Flow Matching方法，将训练样本中的缺失坐标视为潜在变量，通过对所有可能取值进行平均来计算flow matching损失。作者证明了该修正项是精确的而非近似的，并在理论上分析了缺失数据对flow matching学习的影响，以及有限样本下的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow matching通常假设训练数据完全可观测，但真实世界应用中数据缺失十分普遍。现有方法无法直接处理缺失数据场景，因此需要一个能够处理缺失训练数据的flow matching框架。

Method: 将缺失坐标视为潜在变量，在flow matching损失上对其可能取值进行平均化处理。理论上证明在完全随机缺失（MCAR）且真实补全的假设下，不完整数据目标函数与完整数据目标函数等价。对算法留下的设计问题（如每个样本补全次数、估计器方差）进行了有限样本分析，并给出了偏差界。

Result: 理论结果显示：缺失会转移估计器方差而非增加方差；每个样本仅需一次补全即可与完整数据方差匹配；在固定评估预算下，一次补全是最优的。学习型补全模型会引入一个不可约偏差，该偏差由其期望条件Wasserstein距离界定。实验验证了理论预测，并发现确定性插补（而非冻结插补）才是导致生成分布坍缩的原因。

Conclusion: 该工作表明缺失数据的补全模型才是整个流程的难点所在，flow matching本身在精确修正下不受缺失影响。方法在真实表格数据上与强基线方法（经典和深度插补方法）表现相当。

Abstract: Flow matching assumes fully observed training data, which many real-world applications rarely provide. We propose Missing-Data Flow Matching, which treats the missing coordinates of training samples as latent variables and averages the flow matching loss over the values they could take. We first prove the correction is exact rather than approximate. Under missing completely at random with true completions, the incomplete-data objective equals the complete-data objective, so missingness changes nothing about what flow matching learns and the entire difficulty relocates to the completion model. Our finite-sample analysis then answers design questions that the algorithm leaves open, and the answers are not the ones intuition suggests. Missingness transfers estimator variance rather than adding it, one completion per example already matches complete-data variance exactly, and under a fixed evaluation budget one completion is optimal. A learned completion model contributes a single irreducible bias, which we bound by its expected conditional Wasserstein distance to the true completion law. Experiments numerically validate the theoretical predictions, show that deterministic rather than frozen imputation is what collapses the generated distribution, and place our method alongside strong classical and deep imputation baselines on real tabular data.

</details>


### [141] [MMFGU: Multimodal Federated Graph Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28708)
*Haodong Lu,Zekai Chen,Weiwei Ji,Shihao Li,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Yinlin Zhu,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MMFGU框架，解决多模态联邦图学习中的细粒度遗忘请求问题。该框架通过目标特定表示解耦来移除请求的信息，同时保留未请求的内容，并防止目标信息通过剩余模态或图邻域被恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习中，异构多模态内容导致遗忘请求更频繁且更细粒度（如删除特定图片/文本、撤销模态间对应关系等），而现有联邦图遗忘方法主要处理实体/关系或客户端移除，无法直接满足这些多模态请求。

Method: MMFGU框架围绕目标特定表示解耦构建，包含四个核心组件：将异构请求映射为统一目标载体；解耦请求表示同时锚定保留语义；通过轻量探针暴露并修复传播残差；通过紧凑原型和响应信号选择性清除受影响的客户端。

Result: 实验表明MMFGU能有效移除请求信息、保留未请求图数据的效用，并相比完整重训练实现了41.5倍的加速。

Conclusion: MMFGU有效解决了多模态联邦图学习中细粒度遗忘的三个挑战：精准删除而不损害保留内容、防止目标通过剩余信息恢复、阻止相关痕迹通过聚合重新进入全局模型，同时大幅提升了遗忘效率。

Abstract: Multimodal federated graph learning enables clients to collaboratively train graph models over structural, textual, and visual signals without sharing private local data. However, the presence of heterogeneous multimodal content also makes unlearning requests more frequent and fine-grained: users may delete accounts or interactions, remove a particular image or text while retaining the associated entity, or revoke the learned correspondence between retained modalities or graph attributes. Existing federated graph unlearning mainly handles entity/relation or client removal and cannot directly satisfy these multimodal requests. They introduce three challenges: removing only the requested information without damaging retained content, preventing the target from being recovered through remaining modalities or graph neighborhoods, and stopping related traces on other clients from re-entering the global model after aggregation. To address them, we propose \textsc{\textbf{MMFGU}}, a multimodal federated graph unlearning framework built around target-specific representation decoupling. \textsc{MMFGU} maps heterogeneous requests into unified target carriers, decouples requested representations while anchoring retained semantics, exposes and repairs propagated residuals with lightweight probes, and selectively purges affected clients through compact prototype and response signals. Experiments show that \textsc{MMFGU} effectively removes requested information, preserves retained graph utility, and achieves a $\boldsymbol{41.5\times}$ speedup over full retraining.

</details>


### [142] [Mirror Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28737)
*Yunpeng Liu,Matthew Niedoba,Oluwanifemi A. Adekanye,Jason Yoo,Yingchen He,Berend Zwartsenberg,Frank Wood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"镜像学习"框架，通过将第三人称观察转换为第一人称伪专家数据（镜像数据），再利用逆动力学模型推断动作，使智能体能够从被动观察中学习策略，无需遥操作收集数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆（BC）在稠密且对齐的第一人称数据上表现优异，但无法充分利用人类和动物日常依赖的第三人称演示信号。收集第一人称数据通常需要昂贵的遥操作，而第三人称数据（如视频）却大量可得，因此需要一种能跨越视角差距进行模仿学习的方法。

Method: 方法分为两个关键组件：(i) 学习视角变换——使用微调的视频扩散模型将第三人称观察转换到学习者的第一人称视角；(ii) 逆动力学模型——在学习者的控制空间中推断动作轨迹。二者组合生成"镜像数据"，即从第三人称演示合成的伪第一人称专家数据。

Result: 实验表明：仅用镜像数据即可训练有效策略；将镜像数据与第一人称BC训练数据结合可进一步提升下游策略性能。

Conclusion: 现代生成式世界模型隐式编码了足够的结构信息，能够实现可扩展且安全的替代方案，减少对遥操作密集型数据收集的依赖。

Abstract: We investigate imitation learning through the lens of third-person observation and propose a framework for mirror learning: acquiring actionable policies from passive observation. While behavior cloning (BC) excels under dense, well-aligned first-person data, it fundamentally fails to leverage the rich observational signals arising from third-person demonstrations that humans and animals routinely exploit. We introduce a method that composes (i) a learned perspective transformation that places learners in demonstrators' shoes using a fine-tuned video diffusion model and (ii) an inverse dynamics model that infers action trajectories in the learners' control space. This enables the synthesis of mirror data, pseudo first-person expert data generated from third-person observations of demonstrator behavior. Empirically, we show that mirror data alone can train effective policies, and that augmenting first-person BC training with mirror data further improves downstream policy performance. Our results suggest that modern generative world models implicitly encode sufficient structure to enable a scalable and safe alternative to teleoperation-heavy data collection.

</details>


### [143] [TAGTorch: A PyTorch Library for Geometry, Topology, and Symmetry-Aware Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28755)
*Brendan Kennedy,Tegan Emerson,Gregory Roek,Emilie Purvine,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAGTorch 是一个基于 PyTorch 的开源库，旨在统一拓扑、代数和几何启发的深度学习工具，包括数据预处理、网络架构、训练技术和模型分析，弥补当前软件生态系统中这些方法碎片化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，神经网络被应用于越来越多具有丰富几何、拓扑或对称结构的数据，研究者越来越多地从拓扑、代数和几何中汲取灵感。然而，尽管算法发展丰富，支撑这些方法的软件生态系统仍然碎片化，许多重要方法仅以研究原型形式存在于未维护的代码仓库中。

Method: 作者开发了 TAGTorch，一个基于 PyTorch 的开源库，将拓扑、代数和几何启发的工具统一起来，涵盖数据预处理方法、网络架构、训练技术和模型分析工具。论文描述了 TAGTorch 的设计哲学、当前架构和能力，并突出了其在现有软件生态系统中能够填补的空白。

Result: TAGTorch 成功统一了拓扑、代数和几何相关的深度学习工具，弥补了当前软件生态系统的碎片化问题，为研究者提供了集成化的开源解决方案。

Conclusion: 论文以对未来开发优先事项的讨论作为结论，表明该项目将持续发展以进一步丰富和完善这一统一库的功能。

Abstract: Over the last decade, neural networks have been applied to an increasingly diverse range of applications, including data with rich geometric, topological, or symmetry-related structure. As a result, researchers have increasingly drawn inspiration from topology, algebra, and geometry. Despite this rich algorithmic development, the supporting software ecosystem remains fragmented. Many important methods exist only as research prototypes in unmaintained repositories. We address this by introducing Topology, Algebra, and Geometry Torch (TAGTorch), an open-source, PyTorch-based library that unifies tools inspired by topology, algebra, and geometry, including data-preprocessing methods, architectures, training techniques, and model analysis tools. We describe the design philosophy of TAGTorch and then discuss its current architecture and capabilities, highlighting areas where it can fill gaps in the current software ecosystem. We conclude with a discussion of our future development priorities for the library.

</details>


### [144] [Feature Interaction Modeling for Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28762)
*Quan Gu,Hongxia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文受分解机（FMs）启发，将特征交互模块嵌入物理信息神经网络（PINNs）和神经算子学习中，提出FM-PINN、FM-Operator和FM-DeepONet，以增强参数化偏微分方程（PDEs）解流形的模型表达能力，在激波主导方程等挑战性问题上取得了显著的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化PDEs的解流形建模需要捕捉变量之间的耦合关系，而传统的PINNs和神经算子方法在表达变量交互方面存在不足。作者受多元函数二阶泰勒展开启发，认为显式建模时空变量及跨组特征交互可以增强模型表达能力，从而提升对复杂PDEs的逼近精度。

Method: 1) 提出FM-PINN：将分解机中的特征交互模块嵌入PINNs，显式捕获时空变量交互，提升光滑高阶PDEs的逼近精度。2) 将空间坐标、时间、物理参数、初始和边界条件划分为独立特征集，建模跨组交互。3) 在上述策略基础上开发FM-Operator和FM-DeepONet，特别针对非线性守恒律以及具有尖锐梯度或不连续性的问题设计。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的特征交互机制在激波主导等挑战性方程上带来了显著的精度提升，但在光滑算子学习基准上并未体现一致性优势。

Conclusion: 特征交互模块为具有强跨领域依赖性的参数化PDEs建模提供了一个有前景的物理一致性方向，尤其适用于激波等具有不连续性的复杂问题。

Abstract: This work embeds feature interaction modules derived from factorization machines (FMs) into physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) and neural operator learning, to enhance model expressiveness for solution manifolds of parameterized partial differential equations (PDEs). Motivated by the second-order Taylor expansion of multivariate functions to characterize variable couplings, we first propose FM-PINN. It explicitly captures spatio-temporal variable interactions and improves the approximation accuracy for smooth high-order PDEs. We further group spatial coordinates, time, physical parameters, and initial and boundary conditions into independent feature sets and model their cross-group interactions. Based on this strategy, we develop FM-Operator and FM-DeepONet, which are particularly effective for nonlinear conservation laws and problems with sharp gradients or discontinuities, while offering no consistent advantage on smooth operator learning benchmarks. Numerical tests demonstrate that the proposed mechanism delivers substantial accuracy gains on challenging shock-dominated equations, indicating a promising direction for physics-consistent modeling of parameterized PDEs with strong cross-field dependencies.

</details>


### [145] [Representations from Pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials as Coarse Coordinates for Material Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28776)
*Paul Hagemann,Katharina Ueltzen,Simon Müller,Janine George,Philipp Benner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了预训练机器学习原子间势（如MACE）的原子平均特征在无机晶体结构生成中的强大能力。作者提出了粗-细传输距离（CFTD）评估指标，结合质量与新颖性于统一分布评估框架中，并可检测记忆化问题，同时展示了粗MACE特征可引导材料生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的晶体生成模型及其评估方法大多依赖简单的晶体结构表示，缺乏对生成结果质量和新颖性的全面考量。

Method: 提出Coarse-Fine Transport Distance（CFTD）距离度量，采用两种不同的特征提取器，其中质量组件基于粗MACE特征；并进一步利用粗MACE特征作为材料生成模型的引导。

Result: CFTD在捕捉晶体结构质量方面表现出多功能性，同时能够检测记忆化问题，并与近期提出的连续SUN指标进行了比较。

Conclusion: 原子平均的预训练MLIP特征（如MACE）可显著提升晶体生成模型的评估与生成性能，CFTD为材料生成模型的分布级评估提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Generative machine learning is increasingly used for inorganic crystal structure generation. Most models and the corresponding evaluation approaches rely on simple forms of crystal structure representation. In this paper, we showcase the power of atom-averaged features from pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials (MLIPs), such as MACE, for such tasks. We first introduce a distance measure that assesses the output of material generative models by capturing both quality and novelty in a single distribution-based evaluation framework. In particular, we introduce the Coarse-Fine Transport Distance (CFTD) using two different featurizers, where the quality component is based on coarse MACE features. We showcase CFTD's versatility in capturing crystal-structure quality while also detecting memorization, and compare it with the recently introduced continuous SUN metrics. We further show that coarse MACE features can be used as guidance for a material generative model.

</details>


### [146] [Distilling Knowledge from Large Language Models into Lightweight Reinforcement Learning Agents for Autonomous Cyber Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28826)
*Konur Tholl,François Rivest,Mariam El Mezouar,Adrian Taylor,Ranwa Al Mallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大型语言模型（LLM）提升自主网络防御决策能力。通过提示工程而非微调，使用8B参数的安全预训练LLM在CybORG CAGE Challenge 2环境中超越了基线强化学习（RL）智能体，并提出在线策略蒸馏框架将LLM的防御策略迁移到仅64,910参数的轻量级RL智能体中，大幅缩小模型规模同时保持有效防御能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主网络操作（ACO）对企业网络安全防御日益重要，但RL智能体在训练过程中需要大量探索，导致初期决策不稳定且表现不佳。需要寻找更优的防御决策来源来克服RL智能体收敛前的不稳定问题。

Method: 1）使用8B参数、基于网络安全数据预训练的LLM，通过提示工程（而非微调）直接进行防御决策；2）提出在线策略蒸馏框架，将LLM的防御策略迁移到仅64,910参数的轻量级RL智能体中；3）构建4至12台主机的CybORG场景评估可迁移性；4）评估教师引导的RL稳定化策略。

Result: LLM智能体在CybORG CAGE Challenge 2环境中优于基线RL智能体；策略蒸馏成功将LLM策略迁移至轻量级RL智能体，模型规模减少多个数量级同时保持防御能力；教师引导的RL稳定化策略未能持续超越优化后的教师策略，表明奖励驱动的RL优化与教师引导的防御策略之间存在策略对齐局限性。

Conclusion: 网络安全领域的LLM可作为自主网络防御的专业知识来源，而策略蒸馏为实现前沿网络安全模型在轻量级、可部署智能体中的实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous Cyber Operations (ACO) are increasingly important for defending enterprise networks as cyber threats continue to evolve in sophistication. ACO applications commonly employ Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents to learn defensive behaviors through interaction with environments. However, RL agents typically require extensive exploration during training, often resulting in unstable behavior and poor initial decision-making before converging toward effective defense strategies.
  In this work, we investigate the use of a Large Language Model (LLM) to improve autonomous defensive decision-making within an ACO environment. Through prompt engineering rather than fine-tuning, we demonstrate that an 8-billion parameter LLM pretrained on cybersecurity data can outperform a baseline RL agent in a modified CybORG CAGE Challenge 2 environment. We then propose an online policy distillation framework that transfers the LLM's defensive policy into a lightweight RL agent containing only 64,910 parameters, reducing model size by several orders of magnitude while maintaining effective defensive capabilities. This provides a pathway toward operationalizing frontier cybersecurity models within lightweight, deployable agents.
  To evaluate transferability, we construct CybORG scenarios ranging from 4 to 12 hosts and assess the approach across varying network configurations. We also evaluate teacher-guided RL stabilization strategies and observe that none consistently surpass the optimized teacher policy, suggesting policy-alignment limitations between reward-driven RL optimization and teacher-guided defense strategies.
  Our results demonstrate the potential of cybersecurity-focused LLMs as sources of expertise for autonomous cyber defense, while policy distillation provides a practical path toward operationalizing frontier cybersecurity models within efficient, scalable agents.

</details>


### [147] [Hypergradient-based Bilevel Reinforcement Learning with Improved Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28849)
*Naman Saxena,Mudit Gaur,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超梯度的双层强化学习算法，利用熵正则化折扣RL目标下Boltzmann策略的最优性，实现无Hessian计算，达到O(ε⁻¹)的迭代复杂度和Õ(ε⁻²)的最优样本复杂度，并去除了先前工作中外层级目标函数的PL条件假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层强化学习算法要么因使用含Hessian的超梯度而缺乏可扩展性，要么因使用惩罚近似方法而具有高样本复杂度。因此需要一种既高效又低样本复杂度的双层RL算法。

Method: 利用熵正则化折扣RL目标函数下Boltzmann策略的最优性来推导无Hessian的超梯度，从而设计双层RL算法。该方法避免了二阶Hessian计算，并在较温和的正则性条件下进行分析。

Result: 算法实现了O(ε⁻¹)的迭代复杂度和Õ(ε⁻²)的样本复杂度（达到当前最优水平），同时在收敛性分析中成功移除了外层级目标函数的Polyak-Lojasiewicz（PL）条件假设。

Conclusion: 该工作为双层RL提供了一种兼具计算可扩展性和理论最优样本复杂度的新算法，且放宽了理论假设，推动了双层RL在元学习、层级任务分解和RL-HF等领域的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Bilevel reinforcement learning (RL) is an important framework within the literature of RL that can be used to formalize various categories of problems, such as meta-learning, hierarchical task decomposition, and reinforcement learning from human feedback (RL-HF). Most of the bilevel RL algorithms are either not scalable because of using hypergradient with Hessian, or they suffer from high sample complexity because of using penalty-based approximation methods. In this work, we propose a hypergradient-based bilevel RL algorithm using the optimality of the Boltzmann policy for the entropy regularized discounted RL objective function. Our proposed algorithm is Hessian-free and obtains an iteration complexity of $O(ε^{-1})$ and state-of-the-art sample complexity of $\tilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ under mild regularity conditions. Further, in our convergence analysis, we are able to remove the assumption of the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition on the outer-level objective function present in the prior state-of-the-art sample complexity work.

</details>


### [148] [An analysis of machine learning approaches for enhancing decision-making in complex discrete choice tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28854)
*Sheng Lun Christine Cao,Destenie Nock,Alex Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了四种机器学习模型（多项逻辑回归、广义加性模型、孪生神经网络和高斯过程）在离散选择建模中学习和预测五种个体选择规则的能力，通过蒙特卡洛实验和真实能源政策偏好数据案例验证了半参数和非参数模型在政策偏好elicitation中的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散选择建模常用于政策制定中的偏好elicitation，但传统方法依赖参数模型。机器学习有潜力通过数据驱动方式学习个体偏好，然而关于ML方法在个体异质性下估计离散选择规则的能力知之甚少，尤其在偏好elicitation常见的挑战情境下。

Method: 评估四种机器学习模型（多项逻辑回归、广义加性模型、孪生神经网络、高斯过程）学习五种选择规则（线性强效用、单调强效用、理想点、词典序半序、多属性线性弹道累积器）的能力。设计蒙特卡洛实验，系统改变属性数量、训练选择集数量和选择规则的确定性三个维度来评估模型性能，并用真实能源政策偏好数据进行案例研究。

Result: 蒙特卡洛实验显示半参数和非参数模型在所有选择规则和实验情境下普遍优于参数模型。增加训练选择集使模型性能提升6%-96%，增加选择规则确定性使性能提升0%-55%。真实案例中孪生神经网络表现最佳，BIC为13.351。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了半参数和非参数模型在政策导向离散选择建模中的可行性及其局限，并指出选择任务情境应驱动模型选择。

Abstract: Discrete choice modeling is a common tool used for preference elicitation during policy-making, but this is typically done through parametric models. Machine learning can push the boundaries of discrete choice modeling for policy-based preference elicitation by adopting a data-driven approach or learning individual preferences. However, there is limited knowledge of how well machine learning methods can estimate individual discrete choice rules under individual heterogeneity, especially in the context of challenges often experienced during preference elicitation. This study evaluates four machine learning models (multinomial logistic regression, generalized additive model, twinned neural network, and Gaussian process) with respect to their capacity to learn and predict five choice rules that are important in the behavioral and social sciences (linear strong utility, monotonic strong utility, ideal point, lexicographic semiorder, and multiattribute linear ballistic accumulator). Monte Carlo experiments were performed to assess model performance when increasing a) the number of attributes in the choice alternatives, b) the number of training choice sets, and c) the choice rule's determinism. The simulation results demonstrated that semi-parametric and non-parametric models generally outperform parametric models across all choice rules and experimental contexts. Model performance also generally improves by 6% to 96% and 0% to 55%, respectively, with an increase in training choice sets and choice rule determinism. A case study using real energy policy preference data was also conducted, where TNN performed best with a BIC of 13.351. This work demonstrated the viability and limitations of semi-parametric and non-parametric models in the context of policy-centric discrete choice modeling and showed how the choice task context should drive model selection.

</details>


### [149] [Fast Rates for Swap-Agnostic Learning of Proper Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28856)
*Princewill Okoroafor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种交换无关（swap-agnostic）学习的通用框架，通过将预测级别比较转化为联合控制问题，对任意固定的光滑proper loss实现了最优的离线与在线学习率，并扩展到了整个loss族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的agnostic学习允许比较器在每个预测级别上选择不同假设，这虽然能够捕捉预测相关的后处理，但似乎需要为每个预测值分别求解一个agnostic学习问题，计算代价高昂。作者希望找到一种联合控制这些预测级别比较的方法，避免逐值求解。

Method: 核心技术贡献是将交换无关学习归约为一种二阶形式的多元校准（multicalibration），通过Blackwell可逼近性与Berstein风格的方差修正来实现。对于有限假设类H和任意固定的光滑proper loss，这种归约使得所有预测级别的比较可以被联合控制。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对任意固定光滑proper loss，离线超额风险为O~((log|H|/m)^{2/3})，在线交换后悔界为O~(T^{1/3}(log|H|)^{2/3})；(2) 对[-1,1]内所有bounded proper loss，在线和离线率分别为O~(sqrt(T log|H|))和O~(sqrt(log|H|/m))；(3) 对凸的1-Lipschitz proper loss，这些率分别改进到O~(T^{1/3}(log|H|)^{2/3})和O~((log|H|/m)^{2/3})。这些界在log因子级别上是紧的，并优于Luo等人(2025)通过swap-omniprediction保证得到的O~(T^{2/3}(log|H|)^{1/3})界。

Conclusion: 本文证明了swap-agnostic学习可以在整个loss族上同时实现，而不仅仅是单个loss。通过二阶多元校准的归约，作者不仅获得了紧的界限，还展示了这一方法可以同时为多个损失函数提供保证，显著推进了该方向的理论理解。

Abstract: Swap-agnostic learning strengthens classical agnostic learning by allowing the comparator to select a different hypothesis on each level set of the learner's predictions. This benchmark captures prediction-dependent postprocessing, but appears to require solving a separate agnostic-learning problem for every possible prediction value. We show that, for proper losses, these prediction-level comparisons can instead be controlled jointly. Our main result is an offline swap-agnostic learner for any fixed proper loss. For a finite hypothesis class $H$ and any fixed smooth proper loss, the excess risk from $m$ i.i.d. samples is $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$, with a corresponding online swap-regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$. We also give algorithms whose predictions are simultaneously swap-agnostic for entire families of losses. For all proper losses bounded in $[-1,1]$, we obtain online and offline rates of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T\log |H|})$ and $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\log |H|/m})$, respectively. For convex, $1$-Lipschitz proper losses, these rates improve to $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$ online and $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$ offline. These bounds are tight up to logarithmic factors and improve upon the $\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3}(\log |H|)^{1/3})$ rate implied by the swap-omniprediction guarantee of Luo et al. (2025). Our main technical contribution is a reduction from swap-agnostic learning to a second-order form of multicalibration, obtained via Blackwell approachability with a Bernstein-style variance correction.

</details>


### [150] [Adaptivity via a Parallel Architecture for Stochastic Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28902)
*Bin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种并行框架，通过组装静态梯度方法来获得更好的自适应性。该框架利用p个处理器并行搜索适当的迭代次数T（按几何序列排列），使得梯度下降方法满足期望的收敛条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态梯度方法（GD(x0,T)）虽然简单，但其性能高度依赖于迭代次数T的选择，而预先指定T往往无法适应不同问题的特性。作者希望设计一个并行框架来动态搜索合适的T值，从而提升梯度下降方法的自适应性。

Method: 提出一个p处理器并行框架：(1) 步长由s=S(T)决定，其中S是T的预定函数；(2) p个处理器按照几何序列搜索合适的T值；(3) 每个处理器执行由h(j,i)指定的无限阶段序列，在阶段i处理器j执行GD(x0,T_{j,i})；(4) 通过并行搜索，找到满足收敛条件的T值。

Result: 该框架通过并行搜索几何序列中的T值，能够根据实际问题自适应地选择迭代次数，从而获得更好的收敛性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种新的并行梯度方法组装策略，将静态方法通过并行搜索转化为自适应方法，为提升梯度下降的自适应性提供了一种高效途径。

Abstract: We develop a parallel framework that assembles static gradient methods to achieve better adaptivity. A static gradient method, denoted by $\mathrm{GD}(x_0,T)$, takes as input an initial point $x_0\in\mathbb{R}^n$ and $T\in \mathbb{R}^+$ specifying the number $\floor{T}$ of iterations. The step size is chosen as $s=S(T)$, where $S(\cdot)$ is a predetermined function of $T$. The method then performs the iterations $ x_{i+1}=x_i-\fracη{s}\cdot g_i,$ where $g_i$ is a stochastic gradient evaluated at $x_i$, and $η$ is a scaling factor. For an integer $p\ge1$, the $p$ processors in the proposed parallel framework search for an appropriate value of $T$ according to a geometric sequence so that the resulting gradient descent satisfies the desired convergence conditions. Each processor executes an infinite sequence of stages indexed by $i=1,2,\ldots$. At stage $i$, processor $j$ is assigned $ T_{j,i}=h(j,i),$ where $h:\mathbb{N}\times\mathbb{N} \rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{+}$ is a prescribed function. Processor $j$ $(j=0,1,\ldots,p-1)$ executes $\mathrm{GD}(x_0, T_{j,i})$ at stage $i$.

</details>


### [151] [Reflection or Re-Generation? Why LLM Revision Fails Where Human Revision Succeeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28908)
*Yefan Tao,Gerald Friedland,Madhusudhanan Chandrasekaran,Luyang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出人类-LLM反思框架(HRF)，通过控制两遍协议对比人类与LLM在相同条件下的修订行为。发现LLM反思在客观任务上产生接近零的信息增益（类似重新采样），在主观任务上产生显著负增益，而人类修订在两种场景下均产生正增益。根本原因在于：无外部信息的情况下，自条件化修订无法降低关于目标的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被越来越多地提示"反思"，但其反思过程是否真正类人（即基于错误驱动的修订）仍不清楚。现有研究缺乏对人类与LLM在受控条件下修订行为的系统对比，也缺乏从信息论角度量化反思增益的方法。

Method: 构建HRF框架，采用两遍控制协议，在自我、同伴和跨agent三种设定下对比人类与LLM修订；使用基于每轮交叉熵减少的信息论分析（ΔI），并结合诊断分析（按首轮正确性条件化修订、以及oracle引导修订对照随机重排基线）进行机制定位。

Result: 发现LLM反思的两种失败模式：客观任务中ΔI≈0（表现为中性再生，与重采样不可区分）；主观任务中ΔI<0（预测偏离目标）。人类修订在两种设定下均获正增益。跨agent实验表明失败源于修订步骤而非输入质量，LLM甚至会降级高质量的人类回答。进一步诊断发现主导子步骤因任务和模型而异，并非单一机制。

Conclusion: LLM反思本质上应被理解为条件化再生而非真正的错误驱动修订。由于缺乏外部信息，自条件化修订的结构性局限决定了其无法减少关于目标的不确定性，这为理解LLM反思能力的边界提供了统一的理论解释。

Abstract: Reflection, the ability to revisit and revise prior reasoning, is central to how humans improve their answers. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly prompted to "reflect," yet whether this resembles human revision remains unclear. We introduce the Human-LLM Reflection Framework (HRF), a controlled two-pass protocol comparing human and LLM revision under identical conditions across self-, peer-, and cross-agent settings. Using an information-theoretic analysis based on per-iteration cross-entropy reduction, we find two failure modes of LLM reflection. On objective tasks with finite answer spaces, reflection yields near-zero information gain (Delta I approx 0), behaving as neutral re-generation indistinguishable from re-sampling. On subjective tasks, it yields significant negative gain (Delta I < 0), moving predictions away from the target. Human revision, by contrast, yields positive gain in both settings. Cross-agent experiments localize the failure to the revision step, not input quality: LLMs degrade even high-quality human responses. Diagnostic analyses (revision conditioned on first-pass correctness, and oracle-guided revision against a random-reshuffle baseline) show that which sub-step dominates varies by task and by model rather than reducing to a single mechanism: self-error detection is present on objective multiple-choice tasks but weak on subjective ones, and recovery under an oracle error signal exceeds the baseline for some models and falls below it for others. The unifying account is structural: without external information, self-conditioned revision cannot reduce uncertainty about the target, so LLM reflection is better understood as conditioned re-generation than as genuine error-driven revision.

</details>


### [152] [Gated Q-learning: Add Off-Policy Bias to Taste](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28916)
*Brett Daley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gated Q-learning，一种新的Q-learning框架，通过连续的门控机制在Watkins' Q(λ)（无偏但截断）和Peng's Q(λ)（有偏但快速）之间平滑插值，解决了30年来强化学习中多步信用分配与离策略偏置之间的根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步信用分配对样本高效的强化学习至关重要，但Q-learning中管理离策略偏置一直是个根本性挑战。30年来实践者被困在二元选择中：要么消除偏置但严重截断资格迹（Watkins' Q(λ)），要么忽略偏置以加速学习但向价值估计注入有害误差（Peng's Q(λ)）。现代离策略估计器（如重要性采样）无法解决这一矛盾，因为重要性采样比率在Q-learning的贪心目标策略下会崩溃。

Method: Gated Q-learning提出了一种新颖的算法框架，不依赖重要性采样，而是采用连续、状态-动作依赖的门控机制，以探索感知的方式选择性地衰减资格迹（eligibility traces），从而在两个历史极端之间平滑插值。该方法提供了严格的理论基础，证明了期望算子仍是压缩映射（contraction mapping），并推导出其精确的不动点（fixed point）。

Result: 经验评估验证了中间门控值可以安全地实现更长的信用分配视野，产生比两个极端更快的初始学习速度。Gated Q-learning避免了重要性采样带来的方差问题，同时提供了定制有效多步视野和离策略偏置量的灵活性。

Conclusion: Gated Q-learning提供了一种简单而有效的重要性采样替代方案，允许用户自定义Q-learning代理中的有效多步视野和离策略偏置量，在消除传统偏置-方差权衡困境的同时实现更优的学习性能。

Abstract: Multistep credit assignment is critical for sample-efficient reinforcement learning, yet managing off-policy bias in Q-learning remains a fundamental challenge. For 30 years, practitioners have been limited to a binary choice: eliminate the bias at the cost of severely truncated eligibility traces (Watkins' Q($λ$)), or ignore the bias to learn faster while injecting detrimental errors into the value estimates (Peng's Q($λ$)). Modern off-policy estimators fail to resolve this tension, as importance-sampling ratios collapse under Q-learning's greedy target policy. We introduce Gated Q-learning, a novel algorithmic framework that ends this dilemma by smoothly interpolating between the two historical extremes. Rather than relying on importance sampling, our approach employs a continuous, state-action-dependent gating mechanism to selectively attenuate eligibility traces in an exploration-aware manner. We provide a rigorous theoretical foundation for this mechanism, proving that the expected operator remains a contraction mapping and deriving its exact fixed point. Empirical evaluations verify that intermediate gating safely enables longer credit-assignment horizons, yielding faster initial learning than either extreme. Gated Q-learning offers a simple alternative to importance sampling while enabling customization of the effective multistep horizon and the amount of off-policy bias in Q-learning agents.

</details>


### [153] [Learning Optimal Dynamic Matching via Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28925)
*Genta Okada,Shunya Noda,Junpei Komiyama,Akira Matsushita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的动态匹配框架，用于有限演化加权图上的匹配决策问题。核心贡献是证明了事件时间约简定理（最优策略在每次外生事件后立即行动），以及最优边Q函数可由单一延续值函数表征，从而将学习对象从状态-动作值降维为图值。通过图神经网络近似值函数并结合前向贪心匹配启发式，在二元类型基准和肾脏配对捐赠基准上均取得优于即时/阈值贪心策略的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态匹配市场面临"何时匹配"与"匹配谁"的双重决策难题：立即匹配能获得即时价值，但会移除可能在未来创造更好匹配机会的参与者。现有方法缺乏对连续时间、随机到达、节点类型转换、边实现和外生退出等复杂动态环境的系统性价值学习框架。

Method: (1) 建立无限时域连续时间模型，包含随机到达、节点类型转换、边实现和外生退出；(2) 证明事件时间约简：最优策略只需在每个外生事件后立即行动；(3) 推导最优边Q函数可由单一延续值函数（定义在后决策残差图上）完全表征；(4) 用图神经网络近似该值函数，通过时序差分学习训练；(5) 结合前向贪心匹配启发式进行动作选择。

Result: 在二元类型基准测试中，学习到的策略通过保留公共节点以等待稀有高价值匹配到来，同时只在厚市场中形成低价值匹配，显著优于即时和阈值贪心规则。在肾脏配对捐赠基准中：当退出不可预测时性能与即时贪心相当；当预警可靠时恢复患者匹配逻辑；在中间预警概率下超越即时贪心和耐心贪心中表现更好的一方。

Conclusion: 残差图价值学习能够产生适应实际连通性和退出信息的状恶依赖动态匹配策略，在理论和实践层面均验证了该框架的有效性。
